class=\"markdown-body entry-content container-lg\" itemprop=\"text\"\u003e\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eOmarchy bar\u003c/h1\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-omarchy-bar\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Omarchy bar\" href=\"#omarchy-bar\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis is the Quickshell implementation of the Omarchy status bar. It is\nshipped as a first-party plugin of \u003ca href=\"/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/shell/README.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy-shell\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, the\nlong-running shell host. The bar is mounted at startup and lives inside\nthe shell for its whole session.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emanifest.json\u003c/code\u003e declares the plugin (\u003ccode\u003eid: omarchy.bar\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ekind: bar\u003c/code\u003e) and points at \u003ccode\u003eBar.qml\u003c/code\u003e as the entry point.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eBar.qml\u003c/code\u003e is Omarchy-owned bar engine code, loaded by the omarchy-shell host. Users should not edit it directly.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ewidgets/\u003c/code\u003e holds simple first-party bar widgets with sibling manifests.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFeature plugins such as \u003ccode\u003e../panels/audio/\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e../panels/network/\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e../panels/power/\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e../agents/\u003c/code\u003e provide richer popup bar plugins.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe bar receives its config from the host shell as a \u003ccode\u003ebarConfig\u003c/code\u003e property; the host loads it from \u003ccode\u003e~/.config/omarchy/shell.json\u003c/code\u003e (or \u003ccode\u003econfig/omarchy/shell.json\u003c/code\u003e when the user has no file).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy bar position\u003c/code\u003e updates only the user shell.json file.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCustomizing\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-customizing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Customizing\" href=\"#customizing\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe bar config lives under the \u003ccode\u003ebar:\u003c/code\u003e key of \u003ca href=\"/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/shell/README.md#shelljson-shape\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e~/.config/omarchy/shell.json\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e. Out of the box the shell uses \u003ca href=\"/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/config/omarchy/shell.json\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003econfig/omarchy/shell.json\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e. Once you customize anything via the bar gestures, \u003ccode\u003eomarchy bar ...\u003c/code\u003e, or by editing shell.json directly, your file is canonical — there is no deep-merge.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe bar is configured directly on the bar itself: drag empty bar space (or click-and-hold) to move the bar to another screen edge, double-left-click empty center-bar space to toggle transparency, and drag widgets to reorder them. The \u003ccode\u003eomarchy bar position\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eomarchy bar transparent\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eomarchy bar move\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eomarchy bar set\u003c/code\u003e commands do the same from scripts. Enable or disable widgets with \u003ccode\u003eomarchy plugin enable\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eomarchy plugin disable\u003c/code\u003e (widget ids come from \u003ccode\u003eomarchy plugin list\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExample \u003ccode\u003eshell.json\u003c/code\u003e (bar subtree only shown):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\n  \u0026quot;version\u0026quot;: 1,\n  \u0026quot;bar\u0026quot;: {\n    \u0026quot;position\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;top\u0026quot;,\n    \u0026quot;transparent\u0026quot;: false,\n    \u0026quot;centerAnchor\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.clock\u0026quot;,\n    \u0026quot;layout\u0026quot;: {\n      \u0026quot;left\u0026quot;: [\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.menu\u0026quot; },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.spacer\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;size\u0026quot;: 12 },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.workspaces\u0026quot; }\n      ],\n      \u0026quot;center\u0026quot;: [\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.media\u0026quot; },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.clock\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;format\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;HH:mm\u0026quot; }\n      ],\n      \u0026quot;right\u0026quot;: [\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.audio\u0026quot; },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.power\u0026quot; }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"version\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e,\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"bar\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"position\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003etop\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"transparent\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efalse\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"centerAnchor\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.clock\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"layout\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"left\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.menu\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.spacer\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"size\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e12\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.workspaces\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e }\n      ],\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"center\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.media\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.clock\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"format\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eHH:mm\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e }\n      ],\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"right\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.audio\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.power\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecenterAnchor\u003c/code\u003e pins one center module to the exact horizontal/vertical center and flanks others around it. Set to an empty string to disable anchoring (the center list is centered as a group).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" it does\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eInteractions\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.menu\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOmarchy menu launcher\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = menu · right = terminal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.workspaces\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHyprland workspace switcher\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = focus workspace\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.clock\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDate/time label + popup with a month grid, ISO week numbers, and month stepping\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = popup · right = cycle label format · middle = timezone selector\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.media\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMPRIS now-playing — scrolling track + artist, cover-art popup\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = play/pause · middle = next · scroll = prev/next · right = popup\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.indicators\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eManual state indicators\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = indicator action\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.system-update\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAvailable update indicator\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = update\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.tray\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSystem tray\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ehover = reveal drawer · right on chevron = manage\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.weather\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWeather icon + popup with forecast\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = popup · right = full notification\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.microphone\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMic icon + scroll volume\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eleft = mute toggle · middle = audio panel · scroll = source volume\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.audio\u003c/code\u003e | Volume icon + popup with master slider, output-device picker, per-app mixer | left = popup · right = mute · middle = popup · scroll = volume |\n| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.network\u003c/code\u003e | Wi-Fi/Ethernet icon + popup with Wi-Fi scan, signal, connect, DNS provider selection | left = popup |\n| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.tailscale\u003c/code\u003e | Tailscale status, connection switcher, machine browser, and copy actions | left = popup · right = toggle · middle = refresh |\n| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.agents\u003c/code\u003e | AI coding agent limits with pace, today, last week, and all-time model breakdown | left = panel · right = launch agent · middle = next subscription |\n| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.power\u003c/code\u003e | Battery/AC icon + popup with battery stats, power profiles, and system info | left = popup · right = toggle percentage |\n| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.bluetooth\u003c/code\u003e | Bluetooth icon + popup with device list, connect/disconnect, battery | left = popup · right = toggle radio |\n| \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.monitor\u003c/code\u003e | Brightness and laptop display controls | left = popup |\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.indicators\u003c/code\u003e widget loads individual bar indicators from \u003ccode\u003eindicators/\u003c/code\u003e. Omit \u003ccode\u003eitems\u003c/code\u003e (or set it to an empty array) to show all indicators in the default order, or set \u003ccode\u003eitems\u003c/code\u003e to a subset such as \u003ccode\u003e[\"Dnd\", \"Reminder\", \"NightLight\"]\u003c/code\u003e. Set \u003ccode\u003ealwaysShow\u003c/code\u003e to \u003ccode\u003etrue\u003c/code\u003e to keep inactive indicators visible instead of revealing them only on hover. Multiple \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.indicators\u003c/code\u003e instances are allowed, so different sections can show different subsets.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eOrientation\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-orientation\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Orientation\" href=\"#orientation\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll widgets work in \u003ccode\u003etop\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ebottom\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eleft\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eright\u003c/code\u003e positions. Popups anchor on the side opposite the bar edge, sliding into the workspace. Vertical bars use 28px width; widgets that show text fall back to compact icon-only forms (e.g. \u003ccode\u003emedia\u003c/code\u003e hides its scrolling label).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCustom user modules\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-custom-user-modules\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Custom user modules\" href=\"#custom-user-modules\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe schema accepts arbitrary module ids that you provide. Set \u003ccode\u003etype\u003c/code\u003e to \u003ccode\u003ecommand\u003c/code\u003e for shell-driven output or \u003ccode\u003eqml\u003c/code\u003e for a custom QML widget. Both still go under \u003ccode\u003ebar.layout.\u0026lt;section\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e in \u003ccode\u003eshell.json\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCommand module:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\n  \u0026quot;version\u0026quot;: 1,\n  \u0026quot;bar\u0026quot;: {\n    \u0026quot;layout\u0026quot;: {\n      \u0026quot;right\u0026quot;: [\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.tray\u0026quot; },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;vpn\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;type\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;command\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;exec\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;~/.config/omarchy/bar/scripts/vpn-status\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;interval\u0026quot;: 5, \u0026quot;tooltip\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;VPN\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;onClick\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;nm-connection-editor\u0026quot; },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.audio\u0026quot; }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"version\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e,\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"bar\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"layout\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"right\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.tray\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003evpn\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"type\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ecommand\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"exec\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e~/.config/omarchy/bar/scripts/vpn-status\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"interval\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e5\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"tooltip\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eVPN\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"onClick\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003enm-connection-editor\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.audio\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe command may print plain text or Waybar-style JSON, for example:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\u0026quot;text\u0026quot;:\u0026quot;󰌆\u0026quot;,\u0026quot;tooltip\u0026quot;:\u0026quot;Work VPN\u0026quot;,\u0026quot;class\u0026quot;:\u0026quot;active\u0026quot;}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"text\"\u003c/span\u003e:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e󰌆\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e,\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"tooltip\"\u003c/span\u003e:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eWork VPN\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e,\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"class\"\u003c/span\u003e:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eactive\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eQML module:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\n  \u0026quot;version\u0026quot;: 1,\n  \u0026quot;bar\u0026quot;: {\n    \u0026quot;layout\u0026quot;: {\n      \u0026quot;right\u0026quot;: [\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;gpu\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;type\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;qml\u0026quot; },\n        { \u0026quot;id\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;omarchy.audio\u0026quot; }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"version\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e,\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"bar\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"layout\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"right\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003egpu\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"type\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eqml\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e },\n        { \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"id\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy.audio\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen create \u003ccode\u003e~/.config/omarchy/bar/modules/gpu.qml\u003c/code\u003e. If you want to store it elsewhere, add a \u003ccode\u003esource\u003c/code\u003e path.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCustom QML modules should be an \u003ccode\u003eItem\u003c/code\u003e with \u003ccode\u003eimplicitWidth\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eimplicitHeight\u003c/code\u003e. They may optionally define these properties, which the bar fills after loading:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-qml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import QtQuick\n\nItem {\n  property var bar\n  property string moduleName\n  property var settings\n\n  implicitWidth: 28\n  implicitHeight: bar ? bar.barSize : 26\n\n  Text {\n    anchors.centerIn: parent\n    text: \u0026quot;GPU\u0026quot;\n    color: bar ? bar.foreground : \u0026quot;white\u0026quot;\n    font.family: bar ? bar.fontFamily : \u0026quot;monospace\u0026quot;\n    font.pixelSize: 12\n  }\n\n  MouseArea {\n    anchors.fill: parent\n    onClicked: if (bar) bar.run(\u0026quot;omarchy-launch-or-focus-tui btop\u0026quot;)\n  }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e QtQuick\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eItem\u003c/span\u003e {\n  property \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003evar\u003c/span\u003e bar\n  property string moduleName\n  property \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003evar\u003c/span\u003e settings\n\n  implicitWidth\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e28\u003c/span\u003e\n  implicitHeight\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e bar \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e?\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ebar\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ebarSize\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e26\u003c/span\u003e\n\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eText\u003c/span\u003e {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eanchors\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ecenterIn\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e parent\n    text\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eGPU\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n    color\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e bar \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e?\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ebar\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eforeground\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ewhite\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003efont\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003efamily\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e bar \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e?\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ebar\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efontFamily\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003emonospace\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003efont\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003epixelSize\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e12\u003c/span\u003e\n  }\n\n  MouseArea {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eanchors\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003efill\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e parent\n    onClicked\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e (bar) \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ebar\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003erun\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eomarchy-launch-or-focus-tui btop\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e)\n  }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBar properties available to widgets\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-bar-properties-available-to-widgets\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Bar properties available to widgets\" href=\"#bar-properties-available-to-widgets\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWidgets receive \u003ccode\u003ebar\u003c/code\u003e (the shell root), \u003ccode\u003emoduleName\u003c/code\u003e (string), and \u003ccode\u003esettings\u003c/code\u003e (object) injected at load time. The bar exposes:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.foreground\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ebar.background\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ebar.urgent\u003c/code\u003e — theme colors (live-updated)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.fontFamily\u003c/code\u003e — current monospace family\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.position\u003c/code\u003e — \u003ccode\u003e\"top\" | \"bottom\" | \"left\" | \"right\"\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.vertical\u003c/code\u003e — boolean shortcut\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.barSize\u003c/code\u003e — 26 horizontal / 28 vertical\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.run(command)\u003c/code\u003e — fire-and-forget bash exec (quote arguments with \u003ccode\u003eUtil.shellQuote\u003c/code\u003e from \u003ccode\u003eqs.Commons\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.showTooltip(target, text)\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003ebar.hideTooltip(target)\u003c/code\u003e — shared tooltip popup\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebar.requestPopout(owner)\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003ebar.releasePopout(owner)\u003c/code\u003e — one-popup-at-a-time coordinator\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFirst-party bar widgets are manifest-backed just like third-party widgets.\nSimple widgets carry sibling manifests such as \u003ccode\u003ewidgets/Workspaces.manifest.json\u003c/code\u003e;\nricher popup plugins live in feature directories such as \u003ccode\u003e../panels/audio/\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003e../panels/network/\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e../agents/\u003c/code\u003e; and feature plugins such as\n\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.menu\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.media\u003c/code\u003e declare their bar-widget entry points in their own\n\u003ccode\u003emanifest.json\u003c/code\u003e. Bar layout ids are namespaced, e.g. \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.audio\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eomarchy.network\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eomarchy.clock\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThird-party widgets ship as separate plugins under\n\u003ccode\u003e~/.config/omarchy/plugins/\u0026lt;plugin-id\u0026gt;/\u003c/code\u003e with their own \u003ccode\u003emanifest.json\u003c/code\u003e\ndeclaring \u003ccode\u003ekinds: [\"bar-widget\"]\u003c/code\u003e and a \u003ccode\u003ebarWidget\u003c/code\u003e entry point. See\n\u003ca href=\"/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/shell/README.md\"\u003e../../README.md\u003c/a\u003e for the manifest schema. class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Omarchy bar</h1><a id="user-content-omarchy-bar" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Omarchy bar" href="#omarchy-bar"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This is the Quickshell implementation of the Omarchy status bar. It is
shipped as a first-party plugin of <a href="/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/shell/README.md"><code>omarchy-shell</code></a>, the
long-running shell host. The bar is mounted at startup and lives inside
the shell for its whole session.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>manifest.json</code> declares the plugin (<code>id: omarchy.bar</code>, <code>kind: bar</code>) and points at <code>Bar.qml</code> as the entry point.</li>
<li><code>Bar.qml</code> is Omarchy-owned bar engine code, loaded by the omarchy-shell host. Users should not edit it directly.</li>
<li><code>widgets/</code> holds simple first-party bar widgets with sibling manifests.</li>
<li>Feature plugins such as <code>../panels/audio/</code>, <code>../panels/network/</code>, <code>../panels/power/</code>, and <code>../agents/</code> provide richer popup bar plugins.</li>
<li>The bar receives its config from the host shell as a <code>barConfig</code> property; the host loads it from <code>~/.config/omarchy/shell.json</code> (or <code>config/omarchy/shell.json</code> when the user has no file).</li>
<li><code>omarchy bar position</code> updates only the user shell.json file.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Customizing</h2><a id="user-content-customizing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Customizing" href="#customizing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The bar config lives under the <code>bar:</code> key of <a href="/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/shell/README.md#shelljson-shape"><code>~/.config/omarchy/shell.json</code></a>. Out of the box the shell uses <a href="/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/config/omarchy/shell.json"><code>config/omarchy/shell.json</code></a>. Once you customize anything via the bar gestures, <code>omarchy bar ...</code>, or by editing shell.json directly, your file is canonical — there is no deep-merge.</p>
<p dir="auto">The bar is configured directly on the bar itself: drag empty bar space (or click-and-hold) to move the bar to another screen edge, double-left-click empty center-bar space to toggle transparency, and drag widgets to reorder them. The <code>omarchy bar position</code>, <code>omarchy bar transparent</code>, <code>omarchy bar move</code>, and <code>omarchy bar set</code> commands do the same from scripts. Enable or disable widgets with <code>omarchy plugin enable</code> and <code>omarchy plugin disable</code> (widget ids come from <code>omarchy plugin list</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto">Example <code>shell.json</code> (bar subtree only shown):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;version&quot;: 1,
  &quot;bar&quot;: {
    &quot;position&quot;: &quot;top&quot;,
    &quot;transparent&quot;: false,
    &quot;centerAnchor&quot;: &quot;omarchy.clock&quot;,
    &quot;layout&quot;: {
      &quot;left&quot;: [
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.menu&quot; },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.spacer&quot;, &quot;size&quot;: 12 },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.workspaces&quot; }
      ],
      &quot;center&quot;: [
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.media&quot; },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.clock&quot;, &quot;format&quot;: &quot;HH:mm&quot; }
      ],
      &quot;right&quot;: [
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.audio&quot; },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.power&quot; }
      ]
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"version"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"bar"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"position"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>top<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"transparent"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">false</span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"centerAnchor"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.clock<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"layout"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"left"</span>: [
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.menu<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.spacer<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"size"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">12</span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.workspaces<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
      ],
      <span class="pl-ent">"center"</span>: [
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.media<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.clock<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"format"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HH:mm<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
      ],
      <span class="pl-ent">"right"</span>: [
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.audio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.power<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
      ]
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>centerAnchor</code> pins one center module to the exact horizontal/vertical center and flanks others around it. Set to an empty string to disable anchoring (the center list is centered as a group).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Module catalogue</h2><a id="user-content-module-catalogue" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Module catalogue" href="#module-catalogue"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">First-party interactive widgets</h3><a id="user-content-first-party-interactive-widgets" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: First-party interactive widgets" href="#first-party-interactive-widgets"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Name</th>
<th>What it does</th>
<th>Interactions</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.menu</code></td>
<td>Omarchy menu launcher</td>
<td>left = menu · right = terminal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.workspaces</code></td>
<td>Hyprland workspace switcher</td>
<td>left = focus workspace</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.clock</code></td>
<td>Date/time label + popup with a month grid, ISO week numbers, and month stepping</td>
<td>left = popup · right = cycle label format · middle = timezone selector</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.media</code></td>
<td>MPRIS now-playing — scrolling track + artist, cover-art popup</td>
<td>left = play/pause · middle = next · scroll = prev/next · right = popup</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.indicators</code></td>
<td>Manual state indicators</td>
<td>left = indicator action</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.system-update</code></td>
<td>Available update indicator</td>
<td>left = update</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.tray</code></td>
<td>System tray</td>
<td>hover = reveal drawer · right on chevron = manage</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.weather</code></td>
<td>Weather icon + popup with forecast</td>
<td>left = popup · right = full notification</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>omarchy.microphone</code></td>
<td>Mic icon + scroll volume</td>
<td>left = mute toggle · middle = audio panel · scroll = source volume</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">| <code>omarchy.audio</code> | Volume icon + popup with master slider, output-device picker, per-app mixer | left = popup · right = mute · middle = popup · scroll = volume |
| <code>omarchy.network</code> | Wi-Fi/Ethernet icon + popup with Wi-Fi scan, signal, connect, DNS provider selection | left = popup |
| <code>omarchy.tailscale</code> | Tailscale status, connection switcher, machine browser, and copy actions | left = popup · right = toggle · middle = refresh |
| <code>omarchy.agents</code> | AI coding agent limits with pace, today, last week, and all-time model breakdown | left = panel · right = launch agent · middle = next subscription |
| <code>omarchy.power</code> | Battery/AC icon + popup with battery stats, power profiles, and system info | left = popup · right = toggle percentage |
| <code>omarchy.bluetooth</code> | Bluetooth icon + popup with device list, connect/disconnect, battery | left = popup · right = toggle radio |
| <code>omarchy.monitor</code> | Brightness and laptop display controls | left = popup |</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>omarchy.indicators</code> widget loads individual bar indicators from <code>indicators/</code>. Omit <code>items</code> (or set it to an empty array) to show all indicators in the default order, or set <code>items</code> to a subset such as <code>["Dnd", "Reminder", "NightLight"]</code>. Set <code>alwaysShow</code> to <code>true</code> to keep inactive indicators visible instead of revealing them only on hover. Multiple <code>omarchy.indicators</code> instances are allowed, so different sections can show different subsets.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Orientation</h2><a id="user-content-orientation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Orientation" href="#orientation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All widgets work in <code>top</code>, <code>bottom</code>, <code>left</code>, and <code>right</code> positions. Popups anchor on the side opposite the bar edge, sliding into the workspace. Vertical bars use 28px width; widgets that show text fall back to compact icon-only forms (e.g. <code>media</code> hides its scrolling label).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom user modules</h2><a id="user-content-custom-user-modules" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom user modules" href="#custom-user-modules"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The schema accepts arbitrary module ids that you provide. Set <code>type</code> to <code>command</code> for shell-driven output or <code>qml</code> for a custom QML widget. Both still go under <code>bar.layout.&lt;section&gt;</code> in <code>shell.json</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Command module:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;version&quot;: 1,
  &quot;bar&quot;: {
    &quot;layout&quot;: {
      &quot;right&quot;: [
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.tray&quot; },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;vpn&quot;, &quot;type&quot;: &quot;command&quot;, &quot;exec&quot;: &quot;~/.config/omarchy/bar/scripts/vpn-status&quot;, &quot;interval&quot;: 5, &quot;tooltip&quot;: &quot;VPN&quot;, &quot;onClick&quot;: &quot;nm-connection-editor&quot; },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.audio&quot; }
      ]
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"version"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"bar"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"layout"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"right"</span>: [
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.tray<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>vpn<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>command<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"exec"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>~/.config/omarchy/bar/scripts/vpn-status<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"interval"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">5</span>, <span class="pl-ent">"tooltip"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>VPN<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"onClick"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>nm-connection-editor<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.audio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
      ]
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The command may print plain text or Waybar-style JSON, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{&quot;text&quot;:&quot;󰌆&quot;,&quot;tooltip&quot;:&quot;Work VPN&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;active&quot;}"><pre>{<span class="pl-ent">"text"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>󰌆<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"tooltip"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Work VPN<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"class"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>active<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">QML module:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;version&quot;: 1,
  &quot;bar&quot;: {
    &quot;layout&quot;: {
      &quot;right&quot;: [
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;gpu&quot;, &quot;type&quot;: &quot;qml&quot; },
        { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;omarchy.audio&quot; }
      ]
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"version"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"bar"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"layout"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"right"</span>: [
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>gpu<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-ent">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>qml<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> },
        { <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy.audio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
      ]
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then create <code>~/.config/omarchy/bar/modules/gpu.qml</code>. If you want to store it elsewhere, add a <code>source</code> path.</p>
<p dir="auto">Custom QML modules should be an <code>Item</code> with <code>implicitWidth</code> and <code>implicitHeight</code>. They may optionally define these properties, which the bar fills after loading:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-qml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import QtQuick

Item {
  property var bar
  property string moduleName
  property var settings

  implicitWidth: 28
  implicitHeight: bar ? bar.barSize : 26

  Text {
    anchors.centerIn: parent
    text: &quot;GPU&quot;
    color: bar ? bar.foreground : &quot;white&quot;
    font.family: bar ? bar.fontFamily : &quot;monospace&quot;
    font.pixelSize: 12
  }

  MouseArea {
    anchors.fill: parent
    onClicked: if (bar) bar.run(&quot;omarchy-launch-or-focus-tui btop&quot;)
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> QtQuick

<span class="pl-c1">Item</span> {
  property <span class="pl-k">var</span> bar
  property string moduleName
  property <span class="pl-k">var</span> settings

  implicitWidth<span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">28</span>
  implicitHeight<span class="pl-k">:</span> bar <span class="pl-k">?</span> <span class="pl-smi">bar</span>.<span class="pl-smi">barSize</span> <span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">26</span>

  <span class="pl-c1">Text</span> {
    <span class="pl-smi">anchors</span>.<span class="pl-smi">centerIn</span><span class="pl-k">:</span> parent
    text<span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>GPU<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
    color<span class="pl-k">:</span> bar <span class="pl-k">?</span> <span class="pl-smi">bar</span>.<span class="pl-smi">foreground</span> <span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>white<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
    <span class="pl-smi">font</span>.<span class="pl-smi">family</span><span class="pl-k">:</span> bar <span class="pl-k">?</span> <span class="pl-smi">bar</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fontFamily</span> <span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>monospace<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
    <span class="pl-smi">font</span>.<span class="pl-smi">pixelSize</span><span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">12</span>
  }

  MouseArea {
    <span class="pl-smi">anchors</span>.<span class="pl-smi">fill</span><span class="pl-k">:</span> parent
    onClicked<span class="pl-k">:</span> <span class="pl-k">if</span> (bar) <span class="pl-smi">bar</span>.<span class="pl-en">run</span>(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>omarchy-launch-or-focus-tui btop<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)
  }
}</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Bar properties available to widgets</h2><a id="user-content-bar-properties-available-to-widgets" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Bar properties available to widgets" href="#bar-properties-available-to-widgets"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Widgets receive <code>bar</code> (the shell root), <code>moduleName</code> (string), and <code>settings</code> (object) injected at load time. The bar exposes:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>bar.foreground</code>, <code>bar.background</code>, <code>bar.urgent</code> — theme colors (live-updated)</li>
<li><code>bar.fontFamily</code> — current monospace family</li>
<li><code>bar.position</code> — <code>"top" | "bottom" | "left" | "right"</code></li>
<li><code>bar.vertical</code> — boolean shortcut</li>
<li><code>bar.barSize</code> — 26 horizontal / 28 vertical</li>
<li><code>bar.run(command)</code> — fire-and-forget bash exec (quote arguments with <code>Util.shellQuote</code> from <code>qs.Commons</code>)</li>
<li><code>bar.showTooltip(target, text)</code> / <code>bar.hideTooltip(target)</code> — shared tooltip popup</li>
<li><code>bar.requestPopout(owner)</code> / <code>bar.releasePopout(owner)</code> — one-popup-at-a-time coordinator</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">First-party bar widgets are manifest-backed just like third-party widgets.
Simple widgets carry sibling manifests such as <code>widgets/Workspaces.manifest.json</code>;
richer popup plugins live in feature directories such as <code>../panels/audio/</code>,
<code>../panels/network/</code>, and <code>../agents/</code>; and feature plugins such as
<code>omarchy.menu</code> and <code>omarchy.media</code> declare their bar-widget entry points in their own
<code>manifest.json</code>. Bar layout ids are namespaced, e.g. <code>omarchy.audio</code>,
<code>omarchy.network</code>, and <code>omarchy.clock</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Third-party widgets ship as separate plugins under
<code>~/.config/omarchy/plugins/&lt;plugin-id&gt;/</code> with their own <code>manifest.json</code>
declaring <code>kinds: ["bar-widget"]</code> and a <code>barWidget</code> entry point. See
<a href="/basecamp/omarchy/blob/quattro/shell/README.md">../../README.md</a> for the manifest schema. Rescan, enable,
and place third-party plugins with <code>omarchy-shell shell rescanPlugins</code>,
<code>omarchy plugin enable</code>, and <code>omarchy bar move</code>.</p>
</article></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="ScrollMarksContainer-module__scrollMarksContainer__Eu7uU" id="find-result-marks-container"></div><div class="d-none"></div><div class="d-none"></div></div> <!-- --> <!-- --> </div>
</react-app>




  </div>

</turbo-frame>

    </main>
  </div>

  </div>
